1a1(1): @##////rgya gar skad du/shrI dz+nyA nA tsin+t+ya tan+t+ra rA dza/bod skad du/dpal ye shes bsam pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag   
1a2(1): 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das dpa' bo dang mkha' 'gro ma'i 'khor grangs med pa dang /ye shes gsang ba'i gnas ni thabs gcig 
1a3(1): tu bzhugs pa'i dus su dpa' bo ye shes mkha' 'gro zhes bya bas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la spyi bos gtugs te thal mo sbyar nas gsol pa/bcom ldan ye shes mnga' 
1a4(1): bdag che/ye shes kyi ni rang bzhin dang /ye shes skye gnas gang dang gang //rgyal po ji ltar legs pa dang //gang la dbang byed gang gis 'dul//de nyid bdag la  
1a5(1): bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//dpa' bo ye shes mkha' 'gronyon//ye shes zhes bya rnam pa lnga//de nyid don la shes pa dang //log  
1a6(1): rtog shes pas ma bsgribs phyir//spyi bo'i ye shes bshad pa'o//me long ye shes mnyam nyid dang //so sor rtog dang bya nan tan//chos dbyings ye shes lnga pa'o//
1a7(1): de la me long ye shes ni//dper na g.ya' dag me long la//gzugs brnyan gsal bar snang ba bzhin//de bzhin sems kyi me long la//nyon mongs g.ya' ni dag 
1a8(1): byas na|chos rnams gzugs brnyan sgyu mar mthong //sems kyi me long gang yin la//sgyu ma tsam du snang ba de//tha dad mi phyed mnyam ye shes//mnyam 
1b1(1): nyid ye shes gang yin pa//kun rdzob sgyu ma tsam de ni//las dang mtshan nyid ma 'dres pa/de nyid so sor rtog ye shes//de nyid nan tan sgrub byed de//de nyid don grub 
1b2(1): ye shes te//thams cad chos dbyings gzhan min te/de phyir chos dbyings ye shes yin//ye shes skye gnas gang yin pa//kha sbyar dbus kyi rtsa brgyad do//dbyings 
1b3(1): dang ye shes gnyis med tshul//shar dang lho dang nub dang byang //ye shes bzhi po skye ba'i gnas//de dag kun 'dus lte ba ni//chos dbyings ye shes skye ba'i gnas//
1b4(1): me long ye shes mnyam nyid dang //so sor rtog dang bya ba grub//chos dbyings ye shes la sogs pa//bdag po byed pa'i phyir dang ni//zhe sdang dang ni nga rgyal dang //
1b5(1): 'dod chags dang ni phrag dog dang /de bzhin gti mug la sogs pa'i//nyon mongs thams cad 'joms pa'i phyir//rgyal po zhes ni bstan pa yin//ye shes   
1b6(1): mkha'agros yang gsol pa//byang chub sems ni ji ltar lags//de nyid dbye ba gang lags dang //de yi gnas dang gzung ba'i gnas//nges par bzlog 
1b7(1): pa'i tshul nyid dang /de nyid kyi ni 'bras bu dang //rtags dang yon tan gang lags pa//de nyid bdag la bshad du gsol//bcom ldan 'das kyis   
1b8(1): bka' stsal pa//byang chub sems nirang rig ste//dbye ba'i don ni gnyis su bstan//de yi gnas ni rtsa yin te//gzung ba rig pa'i 'khor lor te//
2a1(1): @#//nges par bzlog pa'i thabs dag ni//go bzlog pa yi tshul yin no//de nyid 'bras bu gnyis med rtogs//rtags ni   
2a2(1): lus la khrag byung dang //ba spu langs dang 'dar ba dang //kha zas dang chung lus yang dang //skra dkar gnyer ma dengs pa dang //le lo med dang brtson   
2a3(1): 'grus ldan//de la sogs pa'i rtags rnams 'grub//de nas yon tan bshad pa ni//rkang mgyogs sa steng sa 'og gi/'jig rten dngos grub 
2a4(1): gang yin dang //'jig rten las 'das thob par 'gyur//gnyis med byang chub sems kyi gnas/rang rig 'od gsal de nyid do//ye shes rang 
2a5(1): bzhin gnas yin te//rtags ni 'od gsal de nyid do//'bras bu chos sku 'chang chen pa'i//sa ni nges par thob pa'o//rnal 'byor sems    
2a6(1): gzung sgom pa brtag/lus sems chags su bcug nas ni//rang rig sems kyi de nyid la//de nyid dbyer med bsgom par bya//de yis thob 
2a7(1): med 'bras bu thob//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /ye shes mkha' 'gro la sogs pa'i 'khor thams cad rjes su (yi) rang nas/
2a8(1): de kho na nyid bla na med pa'i mchod pas mchod de mi snang bar gyur to//dpal 'khor lo sdom pa ye shes bsam pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po rdzogs so////
2b1(1): paN+Di ta ga ya d+ha ra dang /lo tsa ba dge slong shAkya ye shes kyis bsgyur ba'o////@#//rgya gar skad du//
2b2(1): shrI hA ra dza tan+t+ra rA dza/bod skad du/dpal chags pa'i rgyal po rgyud kyi rgyal po/dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag 
2b3(1): gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang /gsung dang /thugs rdo rje btsun mo'i b+ha ga la      
2b4(1): bzhugs so//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje snying pos spos dang me tog la sogs pa'i mchod pas mchod nas/he he dpa' 
2b5(1): bo 'jigs pa che//sems can sangs rgyas rang bzhin na//mtshan dang dpe byad gang dang gang /stong dang de bzhin rnam thar dang /rdzus 
2b6(1): skyes mngal skyes sgong skyes dang /drod gsher gnyis gsum bzhi char ci//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//a ni dbu yi gtsug tor 
2b7(1): te;A ni dbu sgra g.yas su 'khyil//i ni dpral ba'i dpyas mnyam la//I ni smin mtshams mdzod pu(sa) ste//u ni spyan mig mthon mthing la//
2b8(1): ba yi rdzi ma lta bu'o//U tshems bzhi bcu mnga' ba ste//r-i r-I tshems mnyam tshems thags bzang //l-i l-I tshems dkar ro bro mchog/e ni 
3a1(1): @#//'gram pa seng ge 'dra//ai ljags shin tu srab pa ste//o ni tshangs pa'i dbyangs dang ldan//au ni dpung mgo shin tu zlum//aM ni 
3a2(1): bdun mtho ba ru gsungs//aHni thal gong rgyas pa ste//'di dag bcu drug g.yas bskor ro//de g.yon bskor mtshams nas kyang //a ni pags srab mdog gser 
3a3(1): 'dra|A ni bzhengs bzhin ma btud par//phyag gnyis pus mor sleb pa'o//i ni ro stod seng ge 'dra//I ni shing n+ya gro lta bur//chu dang zheng ni gab pa'o//u ni    
3a4(1): mdzod spu g.yas su 'khyil//U ni spu rnams gyen du phyogs//ri mdoms sba ba sbubs su nub//rI brla legs par zlum pa'o//l-i ni zhabs long tshigs ma mngon//
3a5(1): l-I ni phyag zhabs 'jam zhing gzhon//l-I ni phyag mthil zhabs kyi mthil//'jam zhing gzhon sha rgyas pa'o//e ni phyag zhabs dra bas 'brel//ai ni sen mo ring 
3a6(1): ba ste|o ni phyag dang zhabs mthil la//'khor lo dang ni ldan pa yin//au ni zhabs ni shin tu gnas//aM zhabs rting pa yangs pa ste//aHni byin pa ri dag sa ni 
3a7(1): e n+ya ya yi lta bu ste//spyi bo'i mi shigs snying po haM/ston pa sum cu rtsa gnyis mtshan//ka ni sen mo zangs mdog 'dra//kha ni sen mo mdog snum pa//ga ni sen 
3a8(1): mo mtho ba ste/g+ha ni sor mo rnams zlum pa//nga ni sor mo rnams rgyas pa//tsa ni sor rnams byin gyis phra//tsa ni rtsa rnams mi mngon pa//dza ni rtsa mdud 
3b1(1): med pa yin//dz+ha ni long bu mi mngon pa//nya ni zhabs mi mnyam pa med//Ta ni seng ge'i stabs su gshegs//ta ni glang bo'i stabs su gshegs//Da ni  
3b2(1): ngang pa'i stabs su gshegs//d+ha ni khyu mchog stabs su gshegs/Na ni g.yas phyogs ldog cing gshegs//ta ni mdzes par gshegs pa ste//tha ni mi g.yo 
3b3(1): bar gshegs pa//da ni sku la 'khril bag chags//d+ha sku byi dor byas pa 'dra//na sku rim par 'tsham pa'o//pa ni sku gtsang pha sku mchog/ba sku rnam par   
3b4(1): dag pa'o//b+ha ni mtshan rdzogs ngo bo nyid//ma sku kho lag yangs shing bzang //ya ni gom pa snyoms pa'o//ra sku shin tu gzhon mdog can//la ni sku la 'khrul pa 
3b5(1): med|wa ni sku lus rgyas pa ste/sha ni shin tu grims pa'o//Sha ni yan lag thams cad mdzes//sa gzigs rab rab med cing dag/ha dgu zlum pa nyid yin te//
3b6(1): k+kha ni dku skabs phyed pa'o//ya dku ma rnyongs pa yin te//ra ni phyal phyam med pa yin//la ni lte ba zab (la)ste//wa ni lte ba g.yas su 'khyil//Da ni kun nas mdzes 
3b7(1): pa ste|D+ha ni kun spyod gtsang ma yin//'di dag thabs kyi rang bzhin gyis//g.yas nas bskor ba bzhi bcu yin//de nas g.yon gyi bzhi bcu yin//de nas yon 
3b8(1): gyi bzhi bcu ni//ka ni sku sme gnag bag med//kha ni shing bal ltar 'jam phyag/ga phyag ri mo mdangs yod pa//g+ha phyag ri mo zab pa dang //nga phyag ri mo ring ba'o//
4a1(1): @#//tsa zhal ha cang mi ring ba//tsha zhal gzugs kyi gzugs brnyan snang //dza dz+ha ljags mnyen ljags srab pa//nya ni ljags dmar 
4a2(1): ngo bo ste|glang po'i nga ro 'brug sgra Ta//Sha ni gsung snyan mnyen 'jam pa//Da D+ha mche ba zlum dang rno//Na ni mche dkar ta tshems mnyam//tha ni mche ba byin gyis phra//
4a3(1): da shangs shin tu mtho ba dang /d+ha shangs shin tu gtsang ba'o//na ni spyan 'od rnam par dag/pa pha spyan bzang rdzi ma ltug/ba ni dkar nag phye pad+ma'i//'dab ma'i     
4a4(1): mdangs 'dra pad+ma'i spyan//b+ha ni de bzhin smin mtshams ring /ma ya smin mtshams smin spu mnyam//ra ni de bzhin smin ma snum//la ni snyan shal stug cing ring //
4a5(1): de bzhin wa ni snyan shal mnyam//snyan gyi dbang po ma nyams sha/dpral ba legs par 'byes pa Sha/sa ni dpral ba'i dbyes che ba//ha ni dbu dag shin tu rgyas//
4a6(1): dbu skra bung ba ltar gnag k+Sha/dbu skra stug pa ya yin te//de bzhin dbu skra 'jam par//dbu skra mi 'dzing ba ni la//dbu skra mi gshor ba ni wa//dbu skra dri zhim 
4a7(1): Da yin te|phyag la dpal gyi be'u dang ni//bkra shis g.yung drung 'khyil bas brgyan//sdong ris lta bu D+ha yin te//lte ba sprul pI snying po aM//gtso bo dpe byed brgyad cur 
4a8(1): ldan|a ni phyi nang stong pa nyid//A ni stong pa nyid stong pa//i ni chen po stong pa nyid//I ni don dam stong pa nyid//u ni 'dus byas stong pa nyid//U ni 'dus ma byas 
4b1(1): stong pa|ri ni mtha' las 'das stong pa//rA ni thog mtha' stong pa nyid//l-i ni dor ba med pa stong //lI ni rang bzhin stong pa nyid//e ni chos rnams thams cad     
4b2(1): stong|ai ni rang gi mtshan nyid ste//o ni mi dmigs stong pa nyid//au ni dngos po med stong nyid//oM ni ngo bo stong pa nyid//dngos med ngo bo nyid aH/mgrin pa 
4b3(1): longs spyod snying po oM/shes rab stong pa bcu drug rtsa//gzugs can gzugs rnams la lta ba//rnam par thar pa dang po a//nang gzugs med par 'du shes pas|
4b4(1): phyi rol gzugs rnams la lta ba/rnam thar gnyis pa ka yin no//sdug pa rnam par thar pa'i lus//mngon sum byas nas rdzogs pa ni/de dag rnam thar gsum 
4b5(1): pa tsa|gzugs kyi 'du shes la 'das pa//sna tshogs 'du shes yid byed//nam mkha' mtha' yas la gnas Ta/nam mkha' mtha' yas 'das te//
4b6(1): rnam shes mtha' yas la gnas shing //rnam shes mtha' yas rdzogs pa ta//rnam shes mtha' yas las 'das na//ci yang med pa la gnas te//de nyid    
4b7(1): rdzogs pa ba yin no//ci yang med pa las 'das te//'du shes med min la gnas te//'du shes med mi rdzogs pa yang //'du shes med min las 'das 
4b8(1): te|'du shes tshor ba 'gog pa'i lus//mngon sum bya sa nas rdzogs pa ni//rnam par thar pa brgyad pa sha/snying ka chos kyi 'khor lo hUM//gtso bo rnam thar 
5a1(1): @#//brgyad dang ldan/'di dag rtsa yi rnam par gnas//'khor lo'i rgya ni sor gsum ste//sems can kun la rang bzhin gnas//
5a2(1): de phyir sems can sangs rgyas yin//sna tshogs sdud pa'i mtshan nyid kyang //ring du 'byung zhing thung du gnas//smin mtshams kyi ni mdzod spu bzhin//chos 
5a3(1): kyi 'byung gnas lte ba la//g.yas g.yon bu ga lnga lnga ste//steng 'og kha yis bar nas 'byung //ro ma shar du mngon du phyogs//brkyang ma nub ste 'og gi  
5a4(1): char;ro mas brkyang ma la 'khyud cing //g.yas pa'i cha ru zung 'jug nyid//brkyang mas ro ma la 'khyud cing //g.yon gyi cha ru zung 'jug nyid//
5a5(1): g.yon rtse mo sku gsung thugs//de sgra sngags su rab tu grags//sngags dang rgyud dang dngos grub sogs//de nyid las ni byung bas na//sems can sangs rgyas 
5a6(1): rang bzhin no//skye ba bzhi yi rim pa yang //snod bcud yab yum la thim ste//yab yum nyi ma zla bar 'gyur//snang ba'i thig le yang dag bcas//'di dag 
5a7(1): nang gi mngal skyes tshul//A li kA li nyi zla ste//sems ni thig le'i ngo bor gnas//nang gi sgong skyes yin par 'dod//ka dang a las nyi zla ste//rang 
5a8(1): byung thig le drod gsher yin//de dag skad cig rkyen med las//rang bzhin 'byung ba rdzus skyes so//rkyen med sbyor ba sgong sogs las//bde ba skye dang 
5b1(1): bde ba'i rgyu//thams cad dran pa gsang ba yi//mngal na skyes pa'i tshul yin te//yar la bsreg dang mar la gso//gnas gsum nyi shu bzhir  
5b2(1): 'pho yin;phyi nang 'dod pa'i mtshan ma yis//rtsa rgyas bde ba ster ba dang //rin po che yi pho brang du//sems 'dzin gsang ba'i sgong skyes yin//nang gi 
5b3(1): 'dod pa'i mtshon cha yis//bde ba skye ba drod gsher skyes//gang gi rkyen gyis ma skyes par//'dod nas bde ba'i rgyur skyes la//bde ba skyes pa rdzus 
5b4(1): skyes so//gtum mo go zlog rkyen gyis sbar//gtum mo rkyen gyis sbar ba dang /gtum mo rang bzhin sbar ba ni//mnga' skyes sgong skyes (drang)gsher skyes//
5b5(1): rdzus skyes rim par sbyar bar bya//du ma'i gzhi bcas gzhi bcas dang /gzhi med shin tu bzhi med de//mngal skyes sgong skyes drod gsher skyes//rdzus skyes 
5b6(1): bzhi char rim par sbyar//rgyu dang lam ste 'bras bu kun//skye ba bzhi yi ngo bor gcig/de phyir sangs rgyas sems can gnyis//dngos po ro mnyam gyur pa la/
5b7(1): log rtog sgyu mas gnyis su 'khrul//rdo rje snying po snying rje che//nang dang gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor yang //nga yis bshad kyis legs par nyon//sku 
5b8(1): yi dkyil 'khor spyi bor sgom/gsuM ni mgrin pa thugs ni snying //yon tan lte ba 'phrin las ni//sna rtse dag tu bsam par bya//'bar ba bzhi a la 
6a1(1): @#//yang|rdo rje phag mo rab tu grags//ha ni bde mchog he ru ka//dpa' bo gcig pa yin par 'dod//bdun pa dang ni dgu pa 
6a2(1): dag|gsum pa hUM las rab tu grags//bcu gsum pa dang bco lnga pa//bcu dgu oM las rab tu grags//nyi shu rtsa lnga nyi shu dgu/haM gi ngo bor yang dag gnas//
6a3(1): sum cu bdun dang drug cu bzhi//a yi dkyil 'khor yin par nges//rtsa ni rdo rje phag mo ste//g.yas g.yon phyag la dbu ma zhal//dpa' bo byang chub sems yin te//
6a4(1): skye mched bcu gnyis dangs ma yang //bcu gnyis phyag tu rab tu grags//'byung bzhi'i dangs ma zhal bzhir nges//mdzes pa'i phyir na rgyan du bshad//gsung mchog 
6a5(1): bla ma'i zhal nas blang //g.yas g.yon steng 'og sgo bzhi ste//'dom gang mnyam pa gru bzhi yin//bdud rtsi lnga ni tshon rtsi lnga//rtsa ni thig gi rang bzhin te//ka ba 
6a6(1): brgyad ni rkang brgyad do//so sen ba spu twa ra sogs//mkhas pas rtag cing shes par bya//'di dag rang bzhin dkyil 'khor te//dper na rang byung gser dang 'dra//
6a7(1): 'di ni bcos ma ma yin te/rang rig ye shes gzugs brnyan ni//gzugs brnyan dkyil 'khor sgom pa min//rdul tshon la sogs bri ba yin//spos la sogs 
6a8(1): pas mchod pa min//rdo shing lha la phyag mi bya//rang bzhin gnas pa'i sangs rgyas la//rang bzhin mchod pas rtag tu mchod//sku ni dgun dus rang bzhin te//
6b1(1): 'byung ba'i sku gsum zla ba gsum//gsung ni dpyid dus ngo bo ste//'jug pa'i sku gsum zla ba gsum/thugs ni dbyar dus ngo bo ste//gnas pa sku gsum 
6b2(1): zla ba gsum;ye shes ston gyi dus yin te//sku gsung thugs ni zla ba gsum//dang po rgyu yi sa pa yin//'khor lo chags pa dmar po la//yi ge rnams 
6b3(1): ni dkar pos gnas/lam gyi sa pa rnam pa gnyis//ring ba dang ni nye ba'o//de ni 'bras bu'i sa pa yin//'khor lo yang dag gnas pa ste//'khor lo de rnam+sa 
6b4(1): lha rnams kyang ;sku mdog phyag mtshan kun dang bral//rang bzhin gsal ba dri ma med//shel ltar gsal ba dri ma med//mi mthong mthong ba'i ngo bo can//
6b5(1): zab cing phra ba'i rang bzhin can//phyi ru sku mdog phyag mtshan lha//nang gi shin tu phra ba'i gzugs//gsang ba'i bde ba lha ru nges//de las bzlog pa 
6b6(1): bzhi pa'i lha//lus las byung phyir lha zhe'am//rgyud rnams dag byed phyir na lha//chags pa'i phyir na lha yin te//rgyal po shes rab rang bzhin te//thabs 
6b7(1): dang shes rab rang bzhin gyi//chags pa chen po'i rgyud gsungs so//de nas sa chen po'ang cher g.yos par gyur te//mchod pa'i sprin gyi char chen po babs par   
6b8(1): gyur to//de nas dpa' bo dang mkha' 'gro ma rnams kyang rgyud kyi don la dga' zhing mgu ste rjes su yi rang nas mi snang bar gyur to//dpal chags pa'i rgyal po'i 
7a1(1): @#//rgyud kyi rgyal po rdzogs so////rnal 'byor ma tsan+d+ra mA le dang /lo tsa ba dge slong shAkya ye shes kyis 
7a2(1): bsgyur ba'o////@##//////rgya gar skad du/sarba ta thA ga ta tsit+ta gar+b+ha ar+tha tan+t+ra/
7a3(1): bod skad du//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs kyi snying po don gyi rgyud//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//'jam dpal gzhon 
7a4(1): nur gyur pa la phyag 'tshal lo//de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad kyi/sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje bdag/snying po mchog ni bzhugs pa'i sku//rdo rje sems 
7a5(1): dpa' la gzigs te/sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes sku//sna tshogs gzugs can sems 'di ni//mtha' dang bral ba yin mod kyi//srid dang 
7a6(1): 'khor bar rab tu grags//de nas rdo rje sems dpa' yis//bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad gsol//kye ho rang byung bcom ldan 'das//
7a7(1): nam mkha'i bdag po sna tshogs lus//gsung gi mnga' bdag rig sngags rgyal//skye med dbyings nas snang byed 'khrungs//'gro drug 'dren pa  
7a8(1): khyod kyi gsung /nyid kyis zhal nas thig pa shor//ke ke ru dang margad dang //spug dang rdo yi snying po dang //rin chen kha dog sna lnga pas//ye 
7b1(1): shes gzhal yas kha dog spas//nyid las mi gzhan 'jigs byed dang //rgyal sras dang pa gtam nyan dang //'gro ba 'dul ba'i 'khor rnams so//
7b2(1): de nas bcom ldan shAka thub kyi//gsung gi dgongs pa mkhyen nas ni//sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes kyi//rang byung ye shes bsdu ba mdzad//
7b3(1): bzhin gshegs kyis 'di skad du//ched du brjod pa rnam par mdzad//gang zhig rtsol med bde chen don//lhan cig skyes pa ma mthong na//sgrub dang 
7b4(1): rtsol ba ci spyad kyang /byis pa don med ga chad du//rdzas bzung ba ni 'ching ba nyid//rten 'brel sgom pa gnyis pa'o//don med nyams myong 'dod pa 
7b5(1): yang /'ching ba'i rgyu ste gsum pa'o//kha cig e ri'i thal bas byugs//dbang phyug nam mkha'i rdzas rnams sdud//'ching ba'i rgyu ste bzhi pa'o//
7b6(1): gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o////mtha' dang bral ba'i rang bzhin la//gzung 'dzin nad kyis ci la 'ching //gdod nas dag 
7b7(1): pa'i nam mkha' la//bag chags mtshan mas ci la 'ching //skye 'gag med pa'i chos nyid la//rgyu 'bras nad kyis ci la 'ching //bsam dang 
7b8(1): bral ba'i ye shes la//rtog dpyod nad kyis ci la 'ching / /e ma'o rnam par dag sems nyid glong //lhan cig skyes pa bde ba'i mchog//
8a1(1): @#//'od gsal sna tshogs dus na bde//rnam dag 'khor lo bde ba la//bsnun pa'i blo gros log pa yis//srid 
8a2(1): rgyu nges pa'i rtsa bar 'khyams//gzhan yang ston pas 'di skad gsungs//zhen chags can gyi gdul bya la//bde ba chen po bcos ma sbyin//khong 
8a3(1): khro can gyi gdul bya la//g.yo ba rlung gi bcos ma sbyin//'dod pa chu bzhin gdungs pa la//bkri drang rang rgyud bcos ma sbyin//kun tu rmongs pa'i 
8a4(1): yid can la//stong pa snying rje bcos ma sbyin//ston pas de skad bka' stsal pa//'khor rnams bsdu ba rnam mdzad de//de bzhin gshegs la 'di

18b2(1): me 'od 'phreng bas yongasu bskor//ye shes rgya mtsho'i 'khor bsrungs ma//shar phyogs mkha' 'gro 'phrog ma dang //lho phyogs glang sna ma zhes bya//nub kyi srin mo ral pa can/byang 
18b3(1): du sa yi gtsug yin no//gang du rgya mtsho chu dang sa dang /nag po'i chu dang 'dra ba'i sku//ma rungs 'joms byed nag po che//'bad pas snying rje bskyed pa nyid//gsad par bya ba 
18b4(1): brjod pa nyid//snying rje med na mi 'grub pas//de phyir snying rje bskyed par bya//mtshams med lnga ni byed pa dang //sangs rgyas bstan pa 'khrug byed pa//zhe sdang rigs kyi ting 'dzin gyis//
18b5(1): re zhig tsam du gsad par bya//gzas pa'i sha la rtag tu rol//kun tu gtum pa'i shugs 'chang ba//yul dang yul du rol byed pas//shing rnams la ni gnas nas kyang //dkyil 'khor 
18b6(1): zhing rnams skyong bar byed//dur khrod gnas kyis yongs su bskor//ma mo'i gnas sogs khyad par byed//klu yis mchod pa'i zhing skyong ba//me yi phye mas yongasu bskor//sgo dang mig gis 
18b7(1): khyad par 'gyur//gtso bo'i sngags kyi lus can te//sgrub pa po yi lus la gnas//de dag rim par dus bstan pa//dam tshig ldan pa'i sngags pa yis//sgrub pa'i rdzas rnams khyad par 
18b8(1): las/'bru nag gtor ma rab tu shis//sha chen gyis ni brgyan pa yi//tshogs rdzas pU dza gus pas dbul//phyi nang gsang ba khyad par las//bstan pa'i tshul gyis 'phrin las las//
19a1(1): @#//bye brag rgyun dang gnas skabs pa//grub dang sgrub pas mngon du 'gyur//dus rnams kun tu zhe sdang gis/rnam pa kun tu gzhom mi bya//yul min pa la bsad byas na//
19a2(1): rnal 'byor dam tshig las nyams phyir//grub pa nam yang yong mi srid//khyad par ye shes bla med pa//dam tshig mgon po sgrub pa yi//dam tshig 'di ni rmad po che//bde bar gshegs pa'i lam   
19a3(1): sbyor mdzad//byang chub sems kyi zla ba 'di//yar ngo mar ngo rab tu gnas//yar ngo yis ni skyes byed la//mar ngo la 'grib par byed//yar ngo yi ni bco lnga la//byang chub sems kyi mkha'agro yang 
19a4(1): bde ba chen por 'du bar mdzad//'di ni nges pa'i don du gsungs//dkyil 'khor lha nyid lus nyid yin//gzhan du lha dang dkyil 'khor ni//btsal yang rnyed par mi 'gyur zhes//
19a5(1): 'dren pa dpa' bos rtag tu gsungs//sku gsung thugs kyi gsang ba la//drag po kun gyi srog gyur pa//yon tan 'phrin las dbang phyug gi/phyi nang gsang ba'i bdag po yin//ston 
19a6(1): pa gnas pa'i rang bzhin las//'khor lo dgug bya gnyis med bstim//dangs snyigs las ni rgyu 'bras dang //gnas pa khyad par rten 'brel te//'khrul dang 'khrul pa ma yin pa//
19a7(1): khams gsum kun la dbang byed pas//'jig rten las dang ye shes kyi//mgon po'i khyad par mngon 'byung ba//gdags pa tha snyad khyab pa po//thun mong bar ni bstan pa yin//
19a8(1): 'di dag las dang skye bo las//sgrib pa thun mong lha yi cha//dngos dang brgyud pas lung bstan pa//srog dang srog ma yin pa ste//rgyu dang rkyen dang bskul ba yis//gsang sngags     
19b1(1): bzlas pa mngon par phye//'di ni mngon du 'byung ba'o//dpal mgon po chen po dngos grub 'byung ba'i rgyud las//gsang ba mngon par 'byung ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////
19b2(1): de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//he ru ka dpal mgon nyid kyi//gsang ba'i sprul pa nag po che//'di yi sgrub rim bshad par bya//gsang ba'i sgrub pa'i tha bas mchog ste//skal  
19b3(1): pa can la sbyin par bya//gang du sgrub pa'i sa phyogs su//mi mthun g.yeng ba spang byas nas//de nas nag po bsgrub par bya//'gro ba kun la rab bltas nas//
19b4(1): tshad med byang chub sems kyi blos//snying rje chen po bskyed byas nas//bde ba'i stan la 'dug nas ni//lhor bltas skra grol gcer bu yis//dang por skad cig 
19b5(1): tsam gyis ni//rang lhar bskyed pa'i snying kha ru//a las zla ba'i dkyil 'khor yang //bsil zhing 'od dang bcas pa bsam//rang steng raM las nyi dkyil 'khor 
19b6(1): rta bdun 'od zer tsha ba dang //me yi rang bzhin 'phro ba bsam//de dbus yi ge hUM nag po//ye shes lnga pa'i rang bzhin can//de yi 'od kyi snang ba las//dpag  
19b7(1): med kha dog bzhi bcas bsam//rang bzhin gnas su 'phros pa las//bla ma dang ni lhag pa'i lha//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo bcas pa dang //nag po chen po   
19b8(1): 'khor dang bcas//mdun gyi nam mkhar bzhugs pa la//skyabs su 'gro dang mchod pa 'bul//sdig pa bshags ba la sogs bya//bzod par gsol nas gshegs su   
21a1(1): @#/ O /dpon du byin gyis brlab po//de nas ral gri blangs te/ral gri ming gis dbang bskur bas dbang bskur la/'di skad ces chos 'di bshad par bya ste/ral gri 'di ni 
21a2(1): rtag bzung la//shes rab tshul 'di dkar smra zhing //nam mkha' sangs rgyas sbyor ba yis//'debs na myur du 'grub par 'gyur//sangs rgyas bstan pa srung phyir dang //sems can mang po bsrung ba'i phyir 
21a3(1): 'dir bsad sdig bar mi 'gyur zhing //bsod nams dag kyang thob par 'gyur//slob ma rnams la 'ang de bzhin shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs pa'i dngos grub rnams kyis myur du bla na med pa yang dag

21a5(1): nge 'dzin 'thob pa la ni/thams cad nam mkha' bsgom pas so//mtshan ma med pa'i ting nge ('dzin)'thob pa la ni/mtshan ma dang bral ba'i ting nge 'dzin bsgoms pas so//smon pa med pa'i ting nge 'dzin thob pa 
21a6(1): la ni/smon pa med pa bsgoms pas so//rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba chos kyi ye shes thob pa la ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu (phyin pa)bsgoms pas so//nyon mongs pa thams cad gcod pa la ni  
21a7(1): chos bsgoms pas so//yi ge thams cad kyi nges tshig gi ye shes la ni/spros pa med pa'i ye shes bsgoms pas so//gnas thams cad du yi ge chos kyi bya ba mthong ba la ni/a la sog+sa 
21a8(1): pa'i yi ge'i ngo bo nyid bsgoms pas so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byang chub thob pa la ni/rdo rje ral gri'i phyag rgyas so//bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog pa'i 
21b1(1): rgyal po chen po las/'khor lo chen po ltar yi ge bskor ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba/rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po/rdzogs so////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
21b2(1): cad kyi 'khor lo'i nang du 'dus pa'i/de bzhin gshegs pas yang 'khor lo chen po'i 'jug pa zhes bya ba shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//rdo rje mnyam pa nyid la 'jug pa'i de bzhin 
21b3(1): gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor la 'jug par 'gyur ro//don gyi mnyam pa nyid la 'jug ba ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'khor lo la 'jug par 'gyur ro//chos thams 
21b4(1): cad kyi mnyam pa nyid la 'jug pa ni/dam pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo la 'jug par gyur ro//thams cad mnyam pa nyid la 'jug pani/'khor lo thams cad la 'jug par 'gyur ro //de nas byang chub 
21b5(1): sems dpa' sems bskyed ma thag tu/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba nyid kyi don 'di rgya cher ston cing /bzhin rab tu 'dzum pas/rdo rje 'khor lo thams cad la/'jug pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i 
21b6(1): snying po 'di smras so///hU~M//de nas 'di gsungs pa tsam gyis/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po can /khams gsum du gtogs pa'i sems can dkyil 
21b7(1): 'khor du zhugs nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi/dam tshig gi sems dpa' rnams nges par byung nas/dkyil 'khor gyi ji lta ba bzhin bzhugs so//
21b8(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje khro bo'i 'khor lo thams cad la rab tu 'jug pa'i phyag rgya zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/dkyil 'khor thams cad la

22a2(1): dbus su rdo rje'i 'khor lo bskor ba bzhag par bya'o//phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' thams cad rnam pa thams cad du/dkyil 'khor dang po'i tshul du bzhag par bya'o//phyi'i 'tshams

22a4(1): phyug gi sku dang /nam mkha'i snying po'i sku rnams so//de nas 'khor lo'i phyag rgya ji lta ba bzhin du/dkyil 'khor thams cad du sangs rgyas nyid dang /rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid du 'grub par 'gyur ro//

22b5(1): su 'dzin pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa mthas cad la mchod pa'i cho gargyas pa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa yi ger 'dri ba las dang /yi ger 'drir 'jug pa dang /'dzin pa dang /klog pa dang /

24b4(1): bcing bar bya zhing bsgrub par bya'o//de nas mchod pa de bzhin du yang dag par tshim par byas nas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul lan cig brjod la slar byung bar bya'o//de bzhin du phyag rgya bcing ba la sogs

24b8(1): thams cad gcad pa la ni/gzugs brnyan byas nas btub pas so//ma rungs pa thams cad dkyil 'khor rnam par gzhig pa la ni//rdo rje gzung nas 'jug par bya'o//mchod pa thams cad rnam 
25a1(1): @#//par gzhig pa la ni/khros nas bzlas brjod bya'o//thams cad bskrad par bya la ni//'jigs byed kyi lta bas blta bar bya'o//bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog
25a2(1): pI rgyal po las/rdo rje dam tshig ces bya ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po/rdzogs so////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa/chos thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid la gnas pas/yang chos 
25a3(1): thams cad kyi mchog ces bya ba/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid pas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa mnyam pa nyid do//chos thams cad don nyid pas 
25a4(1): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa don nyid do//thams cad chos nyid pas/shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa chos nyid do//thams cad las mnyam pa nyid pas/shes rib kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mnyam nyid du rig par bya'o/
25a5(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das lag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi don yod pa'i dam tshig dkyil 'khor byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas/don yod

25a7(1): dam tshig de kho na nyid rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor chen po der zhugs par gyur to //de nas phyag na rdo rjes bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la nges par btud te/dam tshig chen po'i dkyil 'khor chen po 'di gsungs so//
25a8(1): dkyil 'khor chen po bris la/dkyil 'khor gyi gnas brgyad du rdo rje khams gsum las rnam par rgyal ba la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor bzhag go//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje/dam tshig thams 
25b1(1): cad kyi rdo rje la byin gyis brlob pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/dam tshig thams cad kyi rdo rje zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor 'di gsungs so//dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul du phyi'i dkyil bris la/
25b2(1): de'i dbus su/rdo rje'i 'khor lo bris nas/'khor lo'i gnas rnams su/dkyil 'khor chen po la sogs pa bri bar bya'o//thams cad kyang dkyil 'khor brgyad pa'i thig btab cing //sems dpa' chen po bzhag par 
25b3(1): bya'o//phyi'i dkyil 'khor du phyi rol gyi rdo rje rigs ji ltar rjes su thun par bri bar bya'o//sgo rnams su rigs bzhi'i mtshan ma rnams bzhag par bya'o//de nas dam tshig chen po'i de kho na nyid kyi rdo rje dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul
25b4(1): gyis cho ga thams cad rgyas par bya'o//dam tshig chen po'i de kho nanyid kyi dam tshig ces bya ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las/bde ba thams cad kyi dam tshig ces bya ba'irtog pa'i rgyal po chen po//rdzogs s+ho////
25b5(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rnam par snang mdzad kyis//yang sems can thams cad kyi byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya ba/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//byang chub 
25b6(1): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po//thams cad kyi rang bzhin yin pas/sems can thams cad ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po can no//thams cad rdo rje'i snying pos dbang bskur ba nyid pas/sems can
25b7(1): thams cad ni rdo rje'i snying po can no//ngag thams cad rab tu byung bas/sems can thams cad na chos kyi snying po can no//sems can thams cad kyi bya ba byed pa la sbyor bas/sems can thams can kyi las ni snying po can no//
25b8(1): de nas phyi rol gyi rdo rje'i rigs rnams kyi ca co'i sgra phyung nas/chos nyid kyi don rgya cher ston cing snying po phul te/de nas rdo rje dbang phyug chen pos rang gi de kho na nyid snying po 'di phul lo//tri//de nas dkyil 'khor 
26a1(1): @#/ O /bshad par bya ste/kun nas zlum po'i thig gsum pa/dkyil 'khor chen po sgo bzhi pa/'tshams rnams su mdung brtse gsum pa bri bar bya//de'i dbus su dbang phyug chen po bri bar   
26a2(1): bya'o//de'i logs su thams cad du tshogs brgyad po bri bar bya'o//'di lta ste/nag po chen po dang /dga' byed dbang phyug dang /tshogs kyi bdag po dang /gang gi dbang phyug rnams ni phyogs su'o//
26a3(1): 'tshams rnams su thod pa bzang po dang /gtum po'i dbang phyug dang /lag na kha T+wAM ga dang /ba la rna ba'i dbang phyug go/thig gi 'tshams su ni ma mo'i tshogs thams cad dang /gnas ji lta ba bzhin du

26a5(1): slob dpon thal ba gcang mas lus la bsku zhing /go sa gcang ma bgos te/mdung rtse gsum gyi mtshan ma gzhug par bya'o//zhugs nas kyang snying po bzlas par bya'o//de nas slob ma de bzhin
26a6(1): du bcug la/lag par mdung brtse gsum par byin nas chos bshad par bya'o//sems can ('a)du la phan phyir dang /bstan pa thams cad bsrung phyir dang /'dod kun bdag po nyid kyi phyir//
26a7(1): drag po'i las (la)gnas par gyis//de nas phyag rgya'i ye shes blangs te gtor ma sbyin par bya'o//de nas brtsams te/'jigs pa med (par)dngos grub thams cad dang /phun sum tshogs

26b4(1): gyi phyi'i 'tshams rnams su drag po'i mkha' 'gro ma bzhi bri bar bya ste/zhi ba mo dang /be drun ma dang /gtum mo dang /'jigs byed ma rnams so//de rnams kyi mtshan ma ni mdung rtse gsum pa dang /
26b5(1): 'phreng ba dang /'khor lo dang /dril bu dang /thod pa dang /khrag dang /bcas pa dang /khar sil dang /mche ba dang /kha T+wAM ga'o//sgo srungs kyi thod pa dang /rus pa'i lu gu rgyud dang /

26b7(1): rgyas zhugs la snying po bzlas pa bya'o//de nas dkyil 'khor gyi dbus nas/b+h+yo chen po thos par 'gyur ro//de nas slob (ma) gtogs pa gzhan thams cad ji ltar 'ongs pa zhugs par 'gyur zhing thob par

27a1(1): @#/ O /de nas skrag nas me tog gtor ro//de nas gang du lhung ba de lhag pa'i lhar bsgrub par bya'o//gal te lhung bar ma gyur na/yang dor du gzhug go//de nas 'jig 
27a2(1): par byed nus shing /rnal 'byor pa so sor thob par 'gyur ro//de nas gdong g.yogs bkrol la/dkyil 'khor bstan par bya'o//bdug pa la sogs pa'i phyag rgyas mchod nas/snying po rod

27b8(1): bu dang bcas pa'i 'phreng ba rnams g.yo'o//thams cad phab nas de bzhin du gzhug par bya'o//de nas dam tshig bzhin brjod par bya'o//gal te dam tshig 'das gyur na//pha gsod
